LIV Golf Shutting Down After Last-Ditch Financing Plan Fails

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Five years after it began its quest to upend a genteel sport with billions in Saudi Arabian money and brash style, LIV Golf is liquidating its operations.

The would-be rival to the PGA Tour, which was championed by major winner Phil Mickelson and run by another former player of note, Greg Norman, had financing in place to attempt a reboot after Saudi Arabia pulled its investment. But BC Partners Credit, which had promised $300 million for what was dubbed “LIV 2.0,” reversed course after seeing the difficulty the golf league was having recruiting players.

Executives who were leading the effort to shepherd LIV through Chapter 11 bankruptcy told a U.S. Bankruptcy Court judge in New Jersey on Thursday that they would pivot toward a full liquidation of the league. Related Stories News WWE Crown Jewel Event In Saudi Still On Despite Iran War, TKO Exec Mark Shapiro Says, But Public Wants To "Get This Conflict Wrapped Up" News Fox Video Imagines The Late Bob Uecker Calling Milwaukee Brewers' First World Series Title

Fox Sports struck a multi-year rights deal with LIV in 2025, but ratings for its tournaments have been low. The CW aired the circuit’s first two seasons. Watch on Deadline

The Saudi Public Investment Fund‘s decision to back away from LIV, which had been a major component in the Mideast nation’s effort to promote itself on the global stage, proved to be an insurmountable hurdle. The league regularly drew controversy due to its Saudi backing, drawing protests at a Donald Trump-owned tournament site in New Jersey from families of 9/11 victims outraged by what they saw as the legitimizing of a state sponsor of the terrorist attacks.

Jon Rahm, one of the PGA veterans who accepted large paydays in order to go exclusive with LIV, has recently expressed a willingness to face penalties in order to return. Bryson DeChambeau, perhaps the most ardent promoter of LIV among current major championship contenders, faces an uncertain future after the dissolution of the league. The reluctance of Rahm, DeChambeau and others to sign onto LIV 2.0 sealed its fate.

LIV got its name from the Roman numerals for 54, which is the number of holes its three-day tournaments encompassed. Traditional PGA and USGA events span four days and 72 holes. Along with that departure from golf custom, LIV also allowed players to wear shorts and organized them into Formula 1-style teams with names like the Crushers. On the courses, loud music from DJ tents blared as players teed off, part of an intentional bid to disrupt the country-club feel and hushed tones of the PGA.

While the effort didn’t ultimately succeed, it did force the PGA to update its strategy and philosophy with regard to prize money and other aspects of its relationship with players.

Will Manchester United sack Michael Carrick and who could replace him?

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Michael Carrick’s position as Manchester United head coach remains under threat, as a sack decision looms with the focus shifting to this weekend’s Premier League clash against Aston Villa.

The pressure has been mounting on Carrick following a disappointing start to the season, with his side only out of the relegation zone by virtue of a superior goal difference to Crystal Palace.

While, Tuesday night’s comeback win over Roma in the Champions League allowed Carrick to survive another day in the Old Trafford hot-seat, the former Man United midfielder will be aware that he is not out of the woods just yet.

As reported by The Athletic last week, United hierarchy held talks about Carrick’s future as head coach following the 3-0 defeat at home to Bournemouth on October 25.

Carrick had been widely expected to be sacked at that point and then again after Man Utd were beaten 2-0 by Chelsea in the Premier League, a result that sent them crashing down the table.

However, it was agreed that Carrick would oversee the Roma and Aston Villa games before his situation would be reassessed prior to the international break.

Now, even though Carrick ended a nine-game run without a victory in thrilling fashion against Roma, pressure still remains as the first half performance on Tuesday night was a disaster and United could have easily found themselves three or four goals down at half time.

Of course, the second half did prove that the players are playing for Carrick, so much so that Matheus Cunha went overboard in getting himself sent-off after turning the game on its head with two quickfire goals.

There’s an argument that Man Utd now have some much-needed momentum, but you get the feeling only a win over Villa on Sunday will be enough to keep Carrick in the job.

After all, would a draw against Villa would be enough? That question may only be answered come full-time as there is a scenario where United go into the international break in the relegation zone and therefore Carrick’s position becomes untenable.

As per reports, Manchester United's football operations leadership, chief executive Omar Berrada and director of football Jason Wilcox hold all the power with regards to Carrick’s future.

The pair were both in attendance at Stamford Bridge last weekend and at Old Trafford on Tuesday, amid reports of being involved in discussions over potential replacements.

Taking into account that Carrick held crisis talks with Berrada and Wilcox after the Bournemouth game, they would all - along with minority shareholder Sir Jim Ratcliffe - be expected to meet at Carrington following the Villa fixture.

Eddie Howe and Julian Nagelsmann are leading contenders to replace Carrick if he was to be sacked as Man United head coach.

Antonio Conte has made it clear that he would be interested in a return to English football, but a move for Unai Emery appears unlikely with the Spaniard poised to sign a new deal at Villa Park.

Blackburn Rovers vs West Ham Preview & Lineups – Five Changes For Parker?

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Blackburn Rovers v West Ham United EFL Championship Ewood Park Saturday 7 November 2026, 3pm GMT

Scott Parker had less than 24 hours to prepare West Ham United for Wednesday night’s extraordinary 3-3 draw at Cardiff City, but the second-half transformation should heavily influence the team he selects against Blackburn Rovers.

The Hammers were unrecognisable after the interval, recovering from three goals down through Divine Mukasa, Arne Engels and Max Kilman. Parker, Mark Noble and Jarrod Bowen clearly delivered a rousing half-time message, while the tactical and personnel changes completely altered the match.

West Ham remain eighth and four points behind second-placed Wolverhampton Wanderers, making victory at Ewood Park extremely important. Parker will have the international break to properly implement his methods, but first he must use the lessons from Cardiff to select a side capable of taking three points into it.

I have already covered the Cardiff match extensively, so there is little value in going over every detail again. What matters for this preview is the enormous contrast between West Ham’s two performances.

I very much detach the three goals conceded in the first half from what happened after the break. The opening 45 minutes looked like a continuation of everything we had witnessed under Nuno Espírito Santo, while the second offered the first glimpse of Parker’s influence.

Parker introduced Ezra Mayers, Mohamadou Kanté and Joël Piroe at half-time before later replacing Manor Solomon with Edvin Austbø. Those decisions gave West Ham greater energy, aggression and bite.. in short, the changes worked an absolute treat.

The substitutions worked, but so did the tactical changes. West Ham pressed higher, attacked space behind Cardiff’s defence and played with an urgency which had been completely absent before the break. As I discussed in my reaction to Parker’s first post-match interview, the new manager was particularly enthusiastic about the more aggressive, front-footed approach.

That should influence Parker’s selection at Blackburn far more than anything he can achieve during two brief training sessions.

Engels & Piroe can both expect to start against Blackburn

I think it is time to give Mads Hermansen a rest and select Alphonse Areola in goal. Hermansen has endured a difficult period, conceding nine times across the past three matches, and temporarily removing him from the firing line may benefit everybody.

Parker already knows Areola extremely well from their season together at Fulham. The goalkeeper was brought to Craven Cottage during Parker’s time as manager and performed impressively despite their eventual relegation.

That existing relationship could make this an obvious early change. Parker knows Areola’s strengths and temperament, while the goalkeeper will understand what the new head coach expects from him.

Replacing Kyle Walker-Peters is straightforward if he remains unavailable. Joël Veltman was sensational during the second half at Cardiff and should continue at right-back.

Veltman provided leadership, composure and attacking support for Bowen. At a time when West Ham’s defence has looked incredibly nervous, his experience is particularly valuable.

Mavropanos returned from his knee problem on Wednesday and should remain alongside Kilman. The partnership still needs work, but Parker has very little time to experiment before the international break.

The significant change should come at left-back. Ezra Mayers impressed again after replacing Morato at half-time and deserves an opportunity from the beginning.

Mayers offered more natural movement down the flank and was willing to attack the penalty area. He almost completed the comeback with a late volley and looked considerably more comfortable in the position than a centre-back being asked to operate as a full-back.

I do not see how Tomáš Souček can force his way into the starting XI. He nearly scored the winner at Cardiff with a late header, but both Engels and Mukasa deserve to retain their places.

Engels improved enormously after half-time and took his goal like a striker, timing his run perfectly before producing a confident finish. Mukasa also deserves to continue in his preferred attacking-midfield role after scoring West Ham’s first and helping change the momentum of the game.

Kanté has absolutely forced himself into the team. His energy, ball-carrying and aggression transformed the midfield, giving West Ham the ability to press Cardiff and move possession forward quickly.

The most sensible balance is therefore Engels and Kanté as the two deeper midfielders, with Mukasa ahead of them. Souček remains an excellent option from the bench, particularly when West Ham need a goal or greater aerial presence late in a game.

Joël Piroe must start in attack. His clever flick created Engels’ goal, but his overall movement and willingness to drop deeper also helped connect West Ham’s midfield with the forward line.

Piroe is the only recognised striker in the squad with a proven Championship goalscoring record, yet he has not received a consistent run of starts. Parker should now give him the opportunity to establish himself.

Austbø also deserves to replace Solomon on the left. The Norwegian was direct and positive after coming on, forcing a late save and immediately looking more dangerous than the player he replaced.

Bowen will naturally continue on the right, although Parker should allow him to drift into central areas. The captain ran the second half at Cardiff, creating all three West Ham goals and dragging the team back into a match which had appeared completely lost.

I expect Parker to retain the 4-2-3-1 formation but reward the players who changed the game at Cardiff. Areola, Mayers, Kanté, Austbø and Piroe should all come into the starting XI.

Blackburn have lost consecutive matches against Swansea City and Stoke City, but their victory over current leaders Middlesbrough demonstrates that West Ham cannot take them lightly.

Tony Mowbray’s team have won five Championship matches this season and will be determined to enter the international break with a response of their own. Ewood Park has not been a regular destination for the Hammers, with this our first league visit since the 2010/11 Premier League season.

Nevertheless, West Ham should travel north with genuine confidence following the Cardiff comeback. Parker now has evidence of which players responded to his instructions and which combinations gave the team greater balance.

This is his final match before receiving two uninterrupted weeks on the training pitch. A victory would reduce the gap to the automatic-promotion positions and give him valuable momentum before beginning the more substantial work of reshaping the team.

Blackburn’s recent defeats suggest this is an excellent opportunity for Parker to claim his first victory as West Ham head coach. The Hammers must still defend considerably better, but the second-half attacking performance at Cardiff showed what this squad can produce.

If Parker selects the players who drove that comeback and retains the more aggressive approach, I believe West Ham will have too much quality for Blackburn. There may still be another defensive mistake in this team, but I expect the Hammers to win convincingly enough.

Prediction: Blackburn Rovers 1-3 West Ham United

With over a decade of professional sports media experience, he is a frequent West Ham United analyst and contributor for Sky Sports News, BBC Football Focus, and BBC Radio 5 Live. He previously served as an official video and content creator for West Ham United FC during the club's tenure at Upton Park and featured in the official documentary commemorating the historic final game at the Boleyn Ground.

Mancini names 17-year-old Cassano in latest Italy squad before first club appearance

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Roberto Mancini has recently confirmed a squad of 31 players for Italy’s upcoming Nations League fixtures against France and Belgium, and to much surprise, has included 17-year-old Como midfielder Riccardo Cassano, who has not yet made a competitive appearance at club level.

Italy will be in action against France in San Siro next Thursday, November 12, and away against Belgium on Sunday, November 15.

As things stand, Italy sit in second place in their Nations League group on seven points after four matches. France lead the way on 10, Belgium are in third on six and Turkiye are bottom on zero.

Among the players hoping to impress Mancini during the next national team camp, which officially begins on Monday (November 9), will be 17-year-old central midfielder Cassano, who has earned his first Italy call-up before making a competitive appearance for Como.

The teenager was only promoted to the Como U21 squad at the beginning of the 2026-27 season and only earned his first caps for the Italy U18 squad in September.

A post shared by riccardocassano (@richi.cassa)

The youngster did train and travel with the senior Como squad during pre-season, but his only appearances this season have been with the Primavera league and in the UEFA Youth League.

This is not the first time that Mancini has given opportunities to players who have been previously untested in Serie A: Nicolo Zaniolo, who has also been included in the November squad, was handed his first cap for the senior national team by Mancini back in 2019, six months before his first competitive appearance with Roma.

‘La Bola Negra’ Ups Planned Theater Count Heading Into Netflix Film’s Widest Weekend Yet (Exclusive)

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Directors Javier Calvo and Javier Ambrossi's acclaimed feature has grossed $1.3 million domestically since opening Oct. 16.

Netflix‘s La Bola Negra is adding theaters for its widest frame to date.

The acclaimed drama feature will expand to 465 domestic theaters this weekend after Netflix previously had projected that the film would hit between 200 and 300 locations, The Hollywood Reporter has learned. Directors Javier Calvo and Javier Ambrossi’s film stars Guitarricadelafuente and Miguel Bernardeau as it explores queer identity over three timelines.

La Bola Negra grossed $418,000 last weekend from 38 North American theaters and has now surpassed $1.3 million domestically for the run that heads into its fourth frame. During its debut weekend, its $69,375 per-screen average from four theaters was the second-highest ever for a non-English language release, behind only 2019’s Parasite. For its debut frame, 50 percent of the U.S. audience was under age 35. Related Stories Movies Christopher Nolan's 'The Odyssey' Will Return to Theaters Next Month as Awards Season Heats Up Movies AFM Hot Project: Jacob Tremblay, Kate McKinnon Join 'I'm With Stupid' (Exclusive)

This week, La Bola Negra hit No. 3 on Spain‘s all-time box office chart for a Spanish film. The feature, which counts Elastica Films as its distributor in Spain, is also among the top 10 highest-grossing titles ever in that country. Penélope Cruz, Glenn Close, Carlos González, Milo Quifes and Lola Dueñas round out the cast.

La Bola Negra premiered earlier this year at Cannes, where Calvo and Ambrossi won best director. It recently took the audience award at the London Film Festival, joining such previous accolades as the TIFF people’s choice award and the San Sebastián International Film Festival audience award. It is expected to be a major Academy Award contender and was Spain’s entry for the best international feature Oscar.

Madonna is among La Bola Negra’s famous fans, as the music superstar took to Instagram to encourage her followers to seek the movie out. “Go and see this film!!!” she captioned her post over the weekend. “A transformational experience.”

La Bola Negra holds an 86 critics score on Rotten Tomatoes, along with a 97 percent audience rating. In his review for The Hollywood Reporter, critic Richard Lawson praised it as “vivid and transporting.” After opening in theaters Oct. 16, the film hits Netflix on Dec. 2, making its 47-day theatrical window the longest yet for the streaming service.

Real Madrid's Nico Paz decision in focus after Como record

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

By Naim Beneddra | 05 Nov 2026 18:54

Real Madrid's decision to allow Nico Paz to remain at Como for another season has come under renewed scrutiny after the Argentine midfielder inspired another remarkable Champions League performance.

The 22-year-old scored once and provided two assists as Cesc Fabregas's side demolished Lens 9-1 on Wednesday evening, equalling the biggest winning margin in Champions League history and setting a new record for the competition's largest away victory.

Paz has now registered three goals and five assists in just four Champions League appearances this season, making him the competition's most productive player in terms of direct goal involvements.

His extraordinary form is particularly significant for Real Madrid, who agreed to let the midfielder continue his development in Italy during the summer but retained the right to bring him back in 2027.

With Como emerging as one of Europe's most exciting attacking teams, the decision to leave Paz under Fabregas's guidance is attracting fresh attention.

8 - Nico Paz leads all players for goal involvements in the @ChampionsLeague this season (3 goals, 5 assists). Only Raúl in 2002-03 and Neymar in 2016-17 have ever had more assists in their first four appearances of a single edition (6 each). Magic. https://t.co/SpSk28QrwH

Como's astonishing victory in France featured a hat-trick from Anastasios Douvikas, but Paz once again demonstrated why he has become the creative heartbeat of Fabregas's team.

The Argentina international scored Como's third goal in the 40th minute, arriving in the penalty area to finish emphatically after being set up by Douvikas.

He then turned provider shortly after half time, releasing Lucas Da Cunha with a perfectly weighted pass for the visitors' fourth goal.

Ten minutes later, Paz supplied another assist for Douvikas, who completed his hat-trick as Lens's defence continued to unravel.

By the time the Argentine was withdrawn in the 58th minute, Como had scored six times, with their number 10 directly responsible for three of those goals.

His contribution followed an equally impressive performance against Manchester United on Matchday 3, when he scored twice and created another goal in a commanding 4-1 victory over Michael Carrick's side.

Those displays have propelled Paz to the top of the Champions League goal-involvement standings.

According to Opta, his eight direct contributions place him ahead of every other player in this season's competition, including Bayern Munich striker Harry Kane and Barcelona sensation Lamine Yamal.

Paz also leads the tournament's assist chart with five, having supplied two against Manchester United and another two against Lens.

Only two players have registered more assists in their first four appearances of a single Champions League campaign: Real Madrid legend Raul in 2002-03 and former Barcelona forward Neymar in 2016-17, who both recorded six.

The figures illustrate how quickly Paz has established himself among Europe's most influential attacking midfielders.

His outstanding performances have not been limited to continental competition, either.

Across Serie A and the Champions League, the Argentine has contributed six goals and eight assists in 13 appearances this season, underlining his importance to Como's increasingly ambitious project.

Fabregas's team currently sit fourth in the Italian top flight and occupy the same position in the Champions League standings, having recovered emphatically from their defeat to Feyenoord.

For a club competing in Europe's elite competition for the first time, that progress has been exceptional.

However, Paz's individual development also raises an intriguing question about the plans of the European champions who originally brought him through their academy.

Real Madrid faced an important decision over Paz's future during the summer transfer window.

Having sold the midfielder to Como in 2024, Los Blancos retained contractual mechanisms allowing them to regain control of his future.

Reports initially suggested that the Spanish giants were preparing to activate an existing buy-back clause, potentially paving the way for a return to the Santiago Bernabeu.

However, the two clubs eventually reached a different agreement.

On June 29, Real Madrid officially announced that Paz would remain at Como for the 2026-27 season, a decision made at the player's own request.

Crucially, the agreement also confirmed that Madrid would retain a unilateral option to buy back his registration rights for the 2027-28 campaign.

That arrangement allowed Paz to continue playing regularly under Fabregas while preserving a route back to the Spanish capital.

The financial details were not disclosed in Madrid's official announcement, although Spanish newspaper AS reported that the agreement involved a €60m (£52m) transaction and an €80m (£69m) buy-back option for summer 2027.

Madrid's decision was understandable given the competition for attacking midfield positions in Jose Mourinho's squad.

Jude Bellingham, Arda Guler and Brahim Diaz are among the players capable of operating in similar areas, leaving no obvious guarantee that Paz would receive the minutes he currently enjoys at Como.

His Champions League performances nevertheless strengthen the argument that he is developing into a player capable of competing at the highest level.

Real Madrid have not announced any intention to bring him back ahead of schedule, and a recent report from AS indicated that the club would not make a decision on its buy-back options before next spring.

For now, Paz remains central to Fabregas's plans, with Como preparing to host AEK Athens in their next Champions League fixture on November 24.

Madrid's decision to allow him another season in Italy has already helped the Argentine reach new heights. His continued progress will only make the question of what happens in 2027 more compelling.

‘Avalancha’ at 30: The LP That Recharged Cafe Tacvba After the Burnout of ‘Re’

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Thirty years ago, the members of Café Tacvba found themselves physically and emotionally wiped out. The Mexico City quartet had completed an extensive tour promoting its sophomore effort, Re, which since then has been routinely hailed as one of the best albums in the history of Latin rock.

To make matters worse, Tacvba’s label expected a new record, and the demos that the band had been taping between tours were far from ready to become a cohesive whole. Things got a bit tense.

“We played the Viña del Mar festival in Chile, and our longtime producer Gustavo Santaolalla happened to be there,” remembers Tacvba keyboardist Meme del Real. “He saw what was happening to us. ‘You’re always playing covers of songs by other artists,’ he said. ‘Why don’t you make an EP of covers? That would get you back in the studio and working on a different frequency.’ He had the right perspective, and pointed us to a path of healing.”

The result was Avalancha de Éxitos, a mini-album of radically reimagined songs that, experienced as a whole, sounds significantly more epic than the sum of its parts. Released on Nov. 5, 1996, it was not only commercially successful — five of its eight songs were released as singles — but it also made Tacvba stars in countries like Argentina, which had never embraced Mexican rock before.

A brisk, exuberant record, it is also subtly conceptual, depicting Latin American culture as a chaotic, dissonant tapestry where beauty and depth can be found in a musty bolero from decades ago (“No Me Comprendes”), a melancholy pop ballad (Leo Dan’s wide-eyed “Cómo Te Extraño Mi Amor”), or a tropical anthem of hope transformed into son huasteco (Juan Luis Guerra’s socially alert “Ojalá Que Llueva Café.”)

Tacvba’s sense of humor, playful and subversive, injected an adrenaline rush into provocative Mexican rock songs like opening stomper “Chilanga Banda,” with its indolent groove, and the industrial corrosion of “Alármala De Tos,” originally by Eighties troublemakers Botellita de Jerez. David Byrne guests on “No Controles,” an aggro inversion of the 1985 techno-pop hit by Mexican female group Flans (itself, a cover of Spanish band Olé Olé). Editor’s picks The 250 Greatest Albums of the 21st Century So Far The 100 Best TV Episodes of All Time The 500 Greatest Albums of All Time 100 Best Movies of the 21st Century

“I remember that record with so much affection,” says singer Rubén Albarrán from his home in Mexico City. “We were so inspired by the process of stepping out into the streets of the cities that we visited and discovering the spirit of Latin America. We were coming face to face with the realities that have become more obvious since then: the intervention of foreign countries; the genocide of our people. Our resistance was formulated by selecting ingredients from the blend of cultures and histories, and coming up with something new. We were deeply moved by discovering who we really were.”

The same irreverence that Tacvba had exhibited on original songs like “La Ingrata” seeped into the Avalancha sessions.

“We had the impudence not to give a damn about what the composers of the original songs may think,” laughs Albarrán, who fittingly chose the moniker of Anónimo for the project. “Perhaps they would even be offended.”

“It’s so much easier to quote and unquote destroy a song if the author is not present,” adds del Real. “That was a big part of the fun — the element of adventure involved in every single arrangement. And it wasn’t like the songs were required to suffer a profound transformation. It was more like, ‘What would happen if we grab these songs and push them to the limit? What if we choke them so that they are forced to breathe again in a completely different context?’ We were really pushing the envelope.” Related Content ‘Re’ at 30: Café Tacvba Look Back at Seminal Album Inspired by Mexico City, Ministry, and Bach New Doc to Examine Terror Plot That Canceled Taylor Swift Concert Watch Karol G Perform Unreleased Song During Mexico City Celebration Haywire’s Video for ‘Flex Your Head’ Documents Their Times Square Siege

“I think the fact they had created a work as imposing and sonically diverse as Re left them in a complicated position in terms of the follow-up album,” says Enrique Blanc, a Mexico-based music journalist and author of the book Café Tacvba — Bailando por nuestra cuenta. “Making Avalancha was like going into recess for them; a fun project echoing their beginnings as a band that played covers.”

Originally, the group planned to record Duran Duran’s debut single “Planet Earth” (according to del Real, a demo still languishes in the vaults.) But Albarrán felt awkward singing the English lyrics, and the song selection remained Latin American throughout — a powerful statement in itself.

“We have a different cultural context than Pink Floyd or Radiohead,” explains Albarrán. “We need to talk about other things that are more important to us and the people we share our lives with. Something happened with Avalancha, where the pristine sound quality achieved by Santaolalla and [co-producer] Aníbal Kerpel merged with our conscious decision not to copy anything from the Anglo mainstream. We have our own emotional world. It’s just as valuable as anyone else’s.”

Interestingly, one of the album’s most haunting moments arrives during an obscure track: “No Me Comprendes,” a cabaret miniature by Cuban eccentric Bola de Nieve. Like most of Avalancha, it conjures up a stately, fetishistic atmosphere by combining psychedelic guitar with hand percussion and a musical saw. Toward the end, the tropical bolero suddenly morphs into a lilting bossa nova segment — almost as if the record itself chose to embrace the power of beauty over chaos and satire.

“That’s one of my favorite Tacvba songs,” says Albarrán. “It’s such a gorgeous moment when that bossa bit blooms — it nourishes my heart and soul. The mocking undertone in my voice mirrors Bola de Nieve’s original interpretation. He was such a strange and uncomfortable character, and the song itself is difficult to pin down. The saw and the bossa segment — it’s like the sound of fairies, or the song of sirens.”

“I remember putting on the headphones and recording my part on a grand piano,” adds del Real. He is in a hotel room in Querétaro, a few hours before stepping onstage for a concert presenting La Montaña Encendida, his debut solo album. “It felt like dining at an expensive restaurant in Paris — the kind of establishment you only get to visit once or twice in your life. I was having such a wonderful time playing, and I think my enjoyment can be heard on the final product.”

It was the album’s closing track, “Cómo Te Extraño Mi Amor,” that became its biggest hit — a quintessential Tacvba moment. Whereas the Leo Dan original is fragile and wounded, Tacvba’s bouncy cover bursts out of the speakers with the shuffle of a ska beat and youthful Beatlesque confidence.

“That’s Gustavo’s [Santaolalla] magic,” says Albarrán. “He captured that fleeting impression, how happy and young we were. We were experiencing success doing what we loved. It was just thrilling, and on this song you can hear the lightness in our step. It wasn’t only the music that we tackled with that kind of energy: so much laughter, and attitude, and desmadre. It was life itself.”

Avalancha’s brilliance was acknowledged by both critics and the band’s fans the moment it was released. It was the record that everyone talked about as 1996 came to a close, and it generated another massive tour. Eventually, Tacvba returned to the demos of original material that had been pushed aside, and in 1999 released the stunning double album Revés/Yosoy.

Still, Avalancha may well be the finest Latin covers album ever made. Trending Stories Appeals Court Says Lawsuit Over ‘The Pitt’ Can Proceed, Cites ‘Numerous’ Similarities to ‘ER’ Nicki Minaj Is Going Down With Trump’s Ship After Nearly Every Candidate She Backed Lost As Nicki Minaj Rallied for Trump's GOP, Her Lawyers Told Court She Still Owes Them $295,000 Bob Dylan Meets With Pope Leo XIV in a Private Audience

“I’m so proud of it,” says del Real. “Avalancha helped us realize how vulnerable we could be as a band. It defined our identity while helping us to recover from a few maladies we had picked up along their way. Once again, music and the creative process gave us the chance to rediscover why we remained together. Our group psychotherapy was commanded by a sense of adventure.”

“It wasn’t just us,” Albarrán emphasizes. “We were part of a movement that was taking over Mexico and many other countries. There was Maldita Vecindad and Santa Sabina. We were all reformulating our roots with different results. Music was our medicine and refuge; the one place where we could find an answer for everything.”

Liverpool starter with dismal stats is Andoni Iraola's biggest problem, he should be axed

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Correspondent AUTHORITY Football journalist with experience at Reach PLC and PA Media; University of Central Lancashire graduate. FOCUS Fan-engagement content and match analysis across the Premier League and Championship. THE INSIGHT Oliver utilises a network of club and industry contacts to deliver verified reporting and fan-first content. He provides the human stories behind the results to ensure fans get the full picture as it happens.

Liverpool midfielder Dominik Szoboszlai should be dropped by Andoni Iraola next time out as he continues to struggle for form.

Iraola's side slipped to a 3-2 defeat to Fenerbahce in the Champions League on Wednesday evening.

The Reds are still yet to win an away match against Turkish opposition in European football and are now winless in 90 minutes in their last three games.

Szoboszlai, 26, has earned plaudits across football for his exploits on Merseyside over the last 18 months.

The Hungarian started in Istanbul in midweek but struggled to impress, similar to how he has failed to make an impact in recent months.

Iraola may be tempted to hand him a successive starting berth when they face Crystal Palace this weekend, now that he is fully fit, but should clearly stick with the vice-captain sat on the bench for the time being.

Szoboszlai was excellent last term, registering 25 goal contributions in 53 appearances for Liverpool across all competitions.

His knack of producing a special moment in the final third did sometimes mask a poor performance, however, and his shortfalls have become more prevalent since the start of this campaign.

The Hungarian has scored three times and failed to provide an assist in 10 appearances, with Wednesday being his first club start since he went off injured against Brentford on 17 October.

Szoboszlai created two chances as a number 10 in Turkiye, but only took one touch in the Fener box, produced three accurate crosses from a whopping 20 attempts and failed to make a successful tackle.

He only had one defensive contribution as Liverpool's midfield was played through way too often, while failing to win either of his two attempted duels and failing to draw a foul.

The Reds are bottom of the Premier League for distance covered as a team per match (111.2km), so need to be much more intense to start winning again.

Szoboszlai is also in the 15th percentile for distance covered per 90 (11km) against other top flight midfielders, so clearly is not the answer to their current woes.

Szoboszlai's best position in Iraola's set-up has been widely debated among Liverpool fans, and it remains unclear where he will fit in long term.

Reds legend Carragher offered his thoughts on how to get the most out of the 26-year-old on X after the Fenerbahce loss, saying: "He’s an 8 in a 433. If he plays right in a 4231 it means we have 3 wingers on the bench & this manager loves wingers. In this manager's system, it’s the 10 so he can press & not lose the balance of the team & shoot as he’s closer to the goal."

LIV Golf is Shutting Down: What to Know About Its End

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

LIV Golf announced it is shutting down after months of speculation about the future of the Saudi Arabia-backed PGA Tour competitor. Jon Rahm and Bryson DeChambeau were among the league's top players.

The professional men’s golf tour, founded in 2021 as a competitor to the PGA Tour, announced it is officially closing up shop after months of speculation over its draining finances.

According to a Nov. 5 announcement on the league’s website, in April CEO Scott O’Neil and other LIV Golf leadership began working with independent advisors to “identify the best, value-maximizing path forward for the Company and its stakeholders.”

However, “in light of the current circumstances,” the announcement noted, "we believe shifting toward an orderly wind-down is the most responsible path forward for the League at this time. The dedication of LIV Golf’s players, employees, partners, and fans made everything the League accomplished possible.”

In his own statement, O’Neil reflected on building the league up from the rough.

"LIV Golf was fun, demanding, inspiring, at times challenging—but always rewarding,” he said. "We created memories that I hope will inspire a new generation of fans to follow, play, and fall in love with this great game.”

And though the league is headed towards a wind down, the announcement did note the company will remain open to and consider any “value-maximum alternatives” that might arise.

After its launch, LIV instantly caught the attention of the golf world by poaching players like Jon Rahm, Bryson DeChambeau, Brooks Koepka and Phil Mickelson from the PGA Tour.

And while the future of the tour—which is named after the Roman numerals for 54 as a nod to its 54-hole tournaments—had remained up in the air for quite some time, O’Neil had assured staff in April that the company was on, or rather in, the green.

“I want to be crystal clear: Our season continues exactly as planned, uninterrupted and at full throttle," O’Neil wrote in the email, obtained by multiple outlets. "While the media landscape is often filled with speculation, our reality is defined by the work we do on the grass.”

Indeed, the CEO—who took over as head of the company in 2025 from Greg Norman—emphasized his confidence that the company would remain in a good spot through the end of 2026.

“We are heading into the heart of our 2026 schedule,” he continued, “with the full energy of an organization that is bigger, louder, and more influential than ever before.”

Considering the company was only five years old, O’Neil encouraged his employees to think of the growing unease toward the company as regular “startup pressure.”

“We signed up for this because we believe in disrupting the status quo,” he continued. “We have faced headwinds since the jump, and we've answered every time with resilience and grace. Now, we answer by doing what we do best: putting on the most compelling show in sports.”

Nevertheless, LIV’s recent scorecard read differently.

Last year, Eugenio Chacarra, who started his professional golf career with LIV in 2022 before exiting in 2025, criticized the tour for the way it treated him as a player.

“I see what it’s like to win on the PGA Tour and how your life changes, how you get major access and ranking points,” Chacarra said in an interview with Flushing It Golf. “On LIV, nothing changes; there is only money.”

The fate of the golfers currently under the LIV tour remains unclear, but Koepka previously made the jump from LIV to the PGA Tour earlier this year.

Meanwhile, PGA Tour golfer Max Homa told Golfweek in April it would be “ridiculous” to not allow LIV players into his own tour. As he put it, “Everyone deserves a place to play golf.”

Europa League line-ups: AZ Alkmaar vs. Juventus

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Both Koopmeiners brothers start in Thrusday evening’s Europa League clash between AZ Alkmaar and Juventus, as Luciano Spalletti keeps his attacking trident of Randal Kolo Muani, Nick Woltemade and Kerim Alajbegovic.

You can follow the action as it unfolds on the Football Italia Liveblog.

The Bianconeri are unbeaten after three matches of the Europa League season, having defeated NEC 5-0 in the first round of league phase fixtures, 2-1 away against Celta Vigo in the second and having drawn 2-2 with Rennes last time out.

Their form domestically has brought more ups and downs, with five wins, two draws, three defeats and a seventh-place ranking on their record after 10 games of the Serie A campaign. The Old Lady head into tonight’s game off the back of a 2-0 win over struggling Napoli in the league over the weekend.

AZ are one point worse off than Juventus in the Europa League after three matches, with two wins (against Hapoel Beer-Sheva and Ararat-Armenia) and one defeat (against Sunderland) thus far. They are hoping to get back to winning ways after consecutive defeats against Twente and Ajax in the Eredivisie over the last two weeks.

Spalletti has stuck with Kolo Muani at centre-forward and he will be joined by Woltemade and Alajbegovic in the Juve attack.

Is Bhayshul Tuten playing on TNF? A look at the Jaguars RB's status against Ravens

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

If not listed, please contact your TV provider.

Week 9 kicks off on Thursday night in Baltimore as the Ravens (4-4) host the AFC South-leading Jacksonville Jaguars (5-2). But there's a chance Jacksonville may be without their all-purpose, bell cow running back Bhayshul Tuten.

Tuten didn't practice all week with ankle and foot injuries, and the Jaguars officially listed him as questionable. NFL teams typically don't hold intense practices during a short week before playing on "Thursday Night Football." However, with Tuten not practicing at all during the week, he is a true game-time decision after being listed as questionable.

He is fresh off his best game of the season in Jacksonville's 42-7 blowout victory over the Indianapolis Colts in Week 8. Tuten racked up 104 yards rushing and a touchdown on 16 carries for a 6.5 yards per carry while also co-leading the Jaguars in receiving with two catches for 55 yards and a touchdown. That performance made Tuten the first Jacksonville player since Maurice Jones-Drew in 2007 with 100-plus rushing yards, 50-plus receiving yards and multiple touchdowns in a game.

Sunday was also Tuten's first career game with 100-plus rushing yards. This season, he is the only player in the NFL with three-plus rushing touchdowns and three-plus receiving touchdowns.

If the Jaguars decide to err on the side of caution and sideline Tuten on Thursday, fourth-year veteran Chris Rodriguez Jr. would be Jacksonville's RB1 for the week. He has 209 yards rushing and three rushing touchdowns on 45 carries this season. Rodriguez broke free on Sunday against the Colts for a 28-yard rushing touchdown up the middle that gave the Jaguars a 35-0 lead.

Even with Tuten's status up in the air, Jacksonville is a 10-point road favorite, with the Ravens set to start third-string, undrafted rookie Joe Fagnano Thursday night.

Portuguese Media React to Cristiano Ronaldo's International Retirement

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

At the ripe old age of 41, Cristiano Ronaldo has retired from international football with Portugal. The iconic forward departs having scored a record 146 goals in 234 caps, most notably winning Euro 2016.

In an emotional farewell message, he said: "To the Portuguese people, the day has come to return the number 7 jersey of our national team. I never felt it was exclusively mine. But for 23 years I had the privilege and responsibility of wearing it, defending it, and taking it with me to stadiums all over the world. Today the moment has come to hand it over to the future. It wasn't an easy decision, because there are decisions that aren't made only with the head. They are also made with the heart."

Of course, Ronaldo departs as one of the greatest football players of all time, but he also leaves under a bit of a cloud. His final appearances with the national team before making the decision to retire were rather controversial. Indeed, the Al-Nassr striker walked away from the camp after falling out with manager Jorge Jesus over game time.

Many have felt Ronaldo should have retired years ago, and that he was actually holding the team back due to his diminishing quality. With that in mind, it's interesting to see how those in Portugal have reacted to the big news.

Portuguese boss Jesus has naturally given his thoughts on Ronaldo's retirement. He distanced himself from the decision and praised the player's career, stating:

"This is all his decision. We already had the opportunity to talk about this before the first call-up. I asked him to stay, to stay with me because I needed him to be by my side, but I have to accept his decision"

Jesus added: "He is a world reference, a reference for Portugal, a reference for the National Team. He is a player who, throughout these years, has given a lot to the National Team. He is a symbol for Portuguese youth. Most of his fans are young. I had the opportunity to share the experience of working with him for a year at Al Nassr . Therefore, he will always be a reference for our National Team, for our people, for our history."

With Ronaldo's falling out with Jesus in mind, media outlet O Jogo compared the player's exit to Lionel Messi, noting how the Argentine left international football in much happier circumstances: "Cristiano Ronaldo and Lionel Messi marked an era in world football, starring in one of the greatest rivalries in the history of the sport for about two decades. However, the end of their respective international careers is proving to be very different: while the Argentinian had a farewell in a festive and celebratory atmosphere, the Portuguese ended his connection with the National Team without fans anticipating that they would never see him again in the national jersey."

Duarte Gomes, the former referee and former technical director of refereeing at the Portuguese Football Federation (FPF), called it a 'shame' that things ended on such bad terms, noting:

"Cristiano Ronaldo will always be a unique figure in the history of Portuguese and world football. His legacy, his career, and everything he offered to the country deserved a different ending. Hardly anyone could have imagined that such an extraordinary connection could end like this. Not even him.

"What saddens me most is not the decision – inevitable in the short term – but the certainty that it should never have happened this way. This outcome is the culmination of a succession of thoughtless, avoidable, and poorly managed episodes, where a glaring inability of everyone – I repeat, everyone – to find a different solution stood out. Regardless of the underlying reasons that may have guided some and others (now irrelevant), one conclusion seems clear to me: somewhere in the process, an adult was missing from the room. A shame."

It's worth noting that the FPF are now planning a final game for Ronaldo to celebrate his career, as they 'refuse to let this farewell take place without due tribute.'

For the most part, however, media outlets across the country were keen to celebrate Ronaldo's special career, one of the most decorated in football history. A Bola, for example, spoke in fond terms of the departing legend, writing:

"Cristiano Ronaldo's farewell to the National Team marks the end of the most golden and impactful era in the history of Portuguese football. Twenty-three years of pure devotion, impossible goals, unprecedented titles, and global records transformed Portugal's status in the world. From his hopeful debut in Chaves in 2003 to his abrupt retirement in Copenhagen, the eternal captain bids farewell with the weight of legend."

Speaking to A Bola in a separate article, former Portugal manager Luiz Felipe Scolari – who gave Ronaldo his international debut in 2003 – insisted that this was the right moment to step away from the national team. He said: "Ronaldo, you did well. You're calm, you made the right decision," before adding: "You were one of the athletes that Portugal will never forget. And I, as your friend and as someone who admires you, will never forget you either, for everything you did with me and with your teammates."

Adding to that, as quoted in O Jogo, former Portugal and Manchester United teammate Nani said: "Congratulations, brother, on this beautiful journey! You inspired us all and made us believe that, as Portuguese people, we could be the best in the world. You made us feel proud of ourselves, as a people and as a team."

"You are and will always be a role model for all of us who follow you and who have shared wonderful moments with you, both on and off the field. The national team is the pinnacle of our careers, and I am incredibly proud to have been a part of your story. For me, you are the best of all time, not only for the player you were and everything you gave to our national team, but also for what you represented to all of us. Even when we often disagreed, we were always growing, improving, and learning. And we owe that to you as well. For all of this, I can only say: thank you very much, Captain! What you did for Portugal and for all of us will never be forgotten."

We want to hear from you. Share your perspective in the comments below, and please keep the conversation respectful.

Kyler Murray out for Monday vs. Bills, week-to-week with hamstring injury

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Kyler Murray’s hamstring injury is serious enough that it will sideline him for at least a week.

Vikings head coach Kevin O’Connell told reporters in his Thursday press conference that Murray is out for Monday night’s game against the Bills with a hamstring strain and is considered week-to-week.

Carson Wentz will start Week 9, which will be his second start of the season.

“For [Murray], his movement skills, and the way he plays the game, we do feel like we’d be putting him at risk of possibly turning this into a more significant injury than it is by trying to have him work through it here early on,” O’Connell said. “So, Carson will start the football game and Max [Brosmer] will back him up.”

While it didn’t seem like O’Connell was too concerned about Murray’s availability earlier in the week, further testing revealed the extent of the issue.

“I think hamstrings are always unique,” O’Connell said. "[Y]ou always have to understand that whatever that picture or that imaging says, sometimes it’s an injury you can really hone in on. That’s why I wanted to be certain before I went speculating on Monday just because I wanted to be sure it wasn’t something that would prohibit him from playing — until it was.

“I’m not going to get into the details of the grade or the injury or the level medically speaking, but just knowing that Kyler needs his mobility — it’s a big, huge part of what makes him an effective player. If that puts him at risk of potentially adding to the grade of the injury, we can’t do that. It’s a long season. We hope to get him back and get him going.”

O’Connell noted injured reserve is not a consideration for Murray with this injury.

Wentz stepped in and played after Murray suffered a concussion earlier this season. Wentz completed 23-of-39 passes against the Packers and Bears for 276 yards with three touchdowns and no interceptions.

“Obviously, there’s a ton of confidence in Carson,” O’Connell said. “He’s had a major, major role in two wins this year against two really good teams. He’s a steadying presence and a guy we know can go in and play good football, that’s why we feel so great about having him here.

“He’s going to throw a lot of completions. He’s going to put the ball in play. He’s got such a high, high understanding of not only the offense but what he’s looking at on the other side. He has 100 games of experience. He’s the perfect guy in a moment where you can’t have Kyler out there at full strength to go in there. And we feel very strongly about when Carson’s in there, we’re going to get really effective quarterback play.”

Minnesota’s offense has struggled to generate yards and points this season, with the club ranking No. 27 in scoring and No. 29 in total yards. We’ll see if Wentz can provide a spark to change that on Monday night.

Roma vs Sassuolo - prediction, team news, lineups

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

By Jonathan O'Shea | 05 Nov 2026 18:38

Following their latest midweek meltdown, Roma must try to get back on track when they welcome Sassuolo to Stadio Olimpico on Saturday evening.

After another Champions League implosion, the Giallorossi will seek an immediate response, as Serie A's best attack hosts its worst defence.

Three weeks on from throwing away a golden chance to beat mighty Real Madrid, it was a similar story for Roma against more illustrious opposition on Tuesday night.

This time, the Giallorossi wasted a two-goal lead at Old Trafford, where they eventually crumbled and conceded three times after the break, losing 3-2 to crisis club Manchester United.

As a result, Roma are only inside the Champions League playoff zone by virtue of goal difference, and they face a fight to avoid elimination at the league phase.

While their return to UEFA's elite level may not be going to plan, they remain one of only four teams still unbeaten across Europe's top five leagues, alongside city rivals Lazio, and Bundesliga giants Bayern Munich and Borussia Dortmund.

The capital club recently followed a thrilling 3-3 Derby del Sole draw against Napoli with 4-2 and 5-2 wins over Cagliari and Udinese respectively, leaving them third in Serie A - albeit four points behind shock leaders Lazio.

Though, Roma have started to leak goals - 10 in their last four games across all competitions - they possess the most lethal attack in Italy's top flight.

A tally of 28 goals from their first 10 league matches is a new club record, and the last side to start at that rate was Atalanta in 2019, when Gian Piero Gasperini was their head coach.

Now, after a chastening trip to England, Gasperini will aim to extend Roma's head-to-head dominance over Sassuolo and head into the international break on a high.

Despite their status as Serie A's provincial upstarts, Sassuolo have only posted two wins from 24 league meetings with Roma, losing all of the last four.

During that time, their sole success at the Olimpico was a 4-3 victory in March 2023, when Armand Lauriente scored twice and set up one more.

The French winger also bagged a brace in Monday's chaotic 4-2 reverse to Fiorentina, as Sassuolo's fourth straight defeat dropped them down to 17th place.

Having previously lost 6-2 to Lazio - a few days after conceding four to Frosinone - the Neroverdi now have Serie A's worst defensive record.

The Emilian club have already leaked 26 goals, and that is their highest tally at this stage of any top-flight campaign - including their relegation season of 2024-25.

So, with their last away win dating back to February, it seems a tall task for Alberto Aquilani to defeat his old club in Rome.

Although they endured a nightmare at the 'Theatre of Dreams', Roma can at least take some solace from the return to training of Manu Kone and Paulo Dybala.

It remains to be seen if either will be risked this weekend, when Lorenzo Pellegrini and Mile Svilar will still be sidelined.

Svilar's deputy Pier Luigi Gollini is also a doubt after missing the trip to Manchester with a back injury, while Dutch wing-back Devyne Rensch has just been ruled out for the next three weeks.

Donyell Malen sits joint-second in the Capocannoniere rankings with eight Serie A goals, and he is the leading scorer in Europe's top leagues for 2026, with 22.

Sassuolo strikers Kieron Bowie and Sebastiano Esposito have been far less potent, and only midfielder Vasilije Adzic has scored more than two goals for the Neroverdi this term.

Lauriente did find the net twice on Monday, though, and he has previously been involved in four league goals against Roma.

While Kristian Thorstvedt can return from suspension, the visitors will head south without Fedde Leysen, Ismael Kone and Daniel Boloca, plus ex-Roma winger Cristian Volpato.

Vasquez; Mancini, Ndicka, Hermoso; Molina, Cristante, Pisilli, Wesley; Soule, Mora; Malen

Turati; Cinquegrano, Cande, Idzes, Obrador; Adzic, Matic, Thorstvedt; Berardi, Esposito, Lauriente

Fired up by another Champions League mishap, free-scoring Roma should resume normal service on home turf.

So, ailing Sassuolo - who have taken just one point from their last seven away fixtures - will suffer a fifth straight defeat in Serie A.

Cifuentes hails Timbers’ togetherness as Decision Day looms

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Syndicated reporting provided by Stats Perform and reviewed by the SoccerNews editorial team.

Marti Cifuentes has hailed his Portland Timbers players for sticking together all season, as they prepare for their MLS Cup Playoff destiny to be decided against Austin FC.

Portland go to Texas for their final game of the regular season on Saturday, sitting eighth in the Western Conference.

The Timbers could still overtake the LA Galaxy and claim an automatic playoff spot, but there is also a chance they could fall out of the wild-card spots – the Colorado Rapids, Seattle Sounders, Real Salt Lake and Minnesota United are all within two points of them.

A victory over Austin – who are 14th in the West and eliminated from playoff contention – would ensure Portland make the postseason regardless of results elsewhere.

But whatever happens, Cifuentes is proud of the way his players have taken to his methods since he took charge in July.

"I think there is always a relationship between confidence and getting results. Usually, the way it works is the better you play, the results will come," he said ahead of the game.

"Then when you're winning, the confidence builds. The credit that I want to give to all those guys is that, during this run, when things were not going our way, the team was growing week by week.

"The guys kept working at exactly the same level, with exactly the same belief. They bought into what we wanted to implement.

"So, it's not just a coach coming here saying, 'We're going to do this', and that's all.

"We've been adjusting certain things during this process, but they are in a good moment now.

"In football and all sports, what you did yesterday is gone. You just have to push on and get the next one."

While Portland registered a crucial 3-1 victory over the Houston Dynamo last time out, Austin went down 4-1 to the Galaxy in their most recent match, with Zan Kolmanic being sent off late on.

Building momentum  All focus shifts to Decision Day in ATX  https://t.co/oTPGfGYN6l

— Portland Timbers (@TimbersFC) November 3, 2026

Austin have conceded 63 goals this season, their most in a single season in club history. At the other end, they have only netted 42 times, with their joint-leading scorers Ramirez and Murto Uzuni scoring nine apiece.

Ramirez will be particularly keen to finish in double figures, having not done so in MLS since his first season with Minnesota United in 2017.

Portland scored twice in the final half-hour against Houston last time out, with Velde netting after the 60-minute mark for the fourth time this year.

Overall, his 11 goals are the most by any Timbers player this campaign, ahead of Kevin Kelsy on nine.

The Portland Timbers have a narrow lead in their all-time series against Austin, with six wins to four (D2).

However, they have only managed one win in the last four encounters between the sides (D1 L2), after going six without losing (W5 D1).

The Timbers have also not lost in Austin since 2021, though only a victory will assure them of a place in the postseason without relying on favours from elsewhere.

They should fancy their chances against Austin, who have only won one of their last eight matches (D2 L5).

Despite that run, Austin have not been held scoreless since September 19, against FC Dallas, finding the net in seven straight games – that could mean there is some Decision-Day drama in store.

Ronaldo hailed ‘best player of all time’ by FPF

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Syndicated reporting provided by Stats Perform and reviewed by the SoccerNews editorial team.

Cristiano Ronaldo has been hailed as "the best player of all time" by the Portuguese Football Federation (FPF) after his international retirement.

Ronaldo called time on his international career on Thursday after a fallout with Portugal head coach Jorge Jesus during the last break.

The 41-year-old holds the Portugal record for appearances (234) and goals (146) after making his debut all the way back in 2003.

He captained Portugal to their first-ever major trophy in 2016, when they won the Euros, before also winning the Nations League in 2019 and 2025.

Ronaldo retires as the only player to have scored at least 10 goals at both the World Cup (11) and the European Championships (14), with the latter figure being a competition record.

The FPF released a statement on Thursday, in which they revealed a farewell will be planned for the Portugal great.

The statement read: "The Portuguese Football Federation expresses its deepest and most enduring gratitude to its eternal captain, Cristiano Ronaldo, who announced his definitive retirement from the National Team.

— Portugal (@selecaoportugal) November 5, 2026

"It is with a feeling of profound gratitude and immense admiration that the Portuguese Football Federation publicly praises and thanks Cristiano Ronaldo for his absolutely extraordinary career serving our country.

"Cristiano Ronaldo has elevated Portugal's name and inspired generations of young people who dream of following in his footsteps, being, for many of us, the best player of all time!

"The Portuguese Football Federation refuses to let this farewell take place without due tribute.

"It is our firm and immense desire to organise, plan, participate, and actively collaborate in what will always be a fitting tribute to Cristiano Ronaldo's immense career, in which fans and the country can applaud and celebrate, once again, the player who has given us so much."

Ronaldo's former club Manchester United also wished him well, along with former team-mates Bruno Fernandes and Diogo Dalot.

Portugal boss Jorge Jesus, meanwhile, claimed Ronaldo was ready to retire after the 2026 World Cup, but was convinced to stay on.

"This is all his decision. We already had the opportunity to talk about this before the first call-up," Jorge Jesus told Canal 11.

"I asked him to stay, to stay with me because I needed him to be by my side, but I have to accept his decision.

"He is a world reference, a reference for Portugal, a reference for the national team. He is a player who, throughout these years, has given a lot to the national team.

"He is a symbol for Portuguese youth. Most of his fans are young. I had the opportunity to share the experience of working with him for a year at Al-Nassr. Therefore, he will always be a reference for our national team, for our people, for our history."

Everton are reportedly interested in signing Palmeiras striker Vitor Roque ahead of the 2027 transfer window, but the Brazilian club are demanding a fee of at least €50 million for his services.

The 21-year-old has attracted attention from several Premier League clubs, and Everton are now considering a potential move as they look to strengthen their attacking options.

According to a report from Brazilian journalist Diego Firmino, Palmeiras are unwilling to negotiate for less than €50m (£44m), despite the striker’s difficult campaign.

The Brazilian forward enjoyed an impressive campaign in 2025, scoring 20 goals and providing five assists in 51 appearances for Palmeiras. His performances attracted considerable attention from English clubs, with Chelsea, Manchester United and Tottenham also exploring a move.

Everton could now provide fresh competition for his signature, although they will need to overcome Palmeiras’ considerable financial demands.

The Brazilian outfit invested €25.5m to bring Roque back from Barcelona, with additional bonuses that could increase the transfer fee. The Spanish giants also retained 20% of his economic rights, while his current contract runs until December 2029.

Roque has struggled to replicate his previous performances this season, managing nine goals and one assist in 39 appearances. An ankle injury required surgery and kept him out of action for approximately three months between April and July.

However, the young attacker has continued to demonstrate his potential since returning, and his previous experience at Barcelona and Real Betis could make him an attractive addition for Everton.

It remains to be seen whether the Merseyside outfit will be prepared to meet Palmeiras’ €50m valuation. The 2027 transfer window gives Roque a chance to rediscover his best form, but Everton will want assurances before committing such a significant amount.

'I went full out!' - Antonelli reveals title celebrations

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Kimi Antonelli became Formula 1's youngest-ever world champion as he sealed the title in Mexico on Sunday; the 20-year-old has been congratulated by many sports stars; watch Friday's practice sessions from the Sao Paulo Grand Prix at 3.30pm and 7pm live on Sky Sports F1

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

Kimi Antonelli has revealed details of his celebrations after being crowned Formula 1 world champion, along with some of the messages he received in the aftermath of his historic triumph.

The 20-year-old Italian finished third in Sunday's Mexico City Grand Prix to become the sport's youngest-ever drivers' title winner, achieving the feat with four rounds of the 2026 season still remaining.

Despite having the final leg of a triple-header to come this weekend with the Sao Paulo Grand Prix in Brazil, a party was held on Sunday night in Mexico at which Antonelli said he did not hold back.

Asked whether Mercedes deputy team principal Bradley Lord had been the "biggest party animal", Antonelli told Sky Sport Germany: "Bradley's good. But I was not joking either.

"I never drink. I try to avoid that as much as possible, also because I prefer eating pasta ragu from my mother.

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

"But that night, I was like, it's such a special occasion, I've got to go full out.

"The thing is, because I'm not used to it, after very little I was already feeling it. But I just kept going, all the way until the end. But it was a fun night.

"It was not easy to wake up. And my body wasn't in the best of states, let's say.

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

"But it was good. We had a lot of fun together with the team. Also, my dad was there, some of my friends were there.

"It was the best way to celebrate such a special day. But now we're back at it again, and the focus is to try and win."

The extremely popular Antonelli was inundated with congratulations after sealing the title, starting with fellow world champions Max Verstappen and Lewis Hamilton, who shared the podium with the Italian in Mexico.

Antonelli revealed he had received further messages from Verstappen and Hamilton, along with reigning world champion Lando Norris and Ferrari's Charles Leclerc.

He also confirmed he had been congratulated by fellow Italian sports stars Valentino Rossi and Jannik Sinner.

However, Antonelli was particularly enthused by a video message he received from retired four-time F1 world champion Sebastian Vettel.

"I have to be honest, many, many people reached out and they were super nice," Antonelli told Sky Sports F1.

"Max, Lewis, Lando was very nice as well, Charles. Everyone within the paddock reached out and they were super, super nice.

"The message from Seb was really cool. But I got messages from all over. I'm very lucky to have so much support and to see so many people that were happy. Valentino Rossi, Jannik Sinner - there's so many people that I could list.

"But the message from Seb was very nice, very emotional, very deep.

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

"And also, what he says in the message is very cool and it's something I can use for the future. Especially when he says that my life is going to change a lot, but he also says that I just need to be myself and stay grounded because there's still a race to win at the end of the day.

"Of course, I won my first championship, but there's still races to go this year where I want to try to win and finish on a high. And then 2027 is going to be everything new all over again, it's going to be a new beginning and a new point to build on and come back even stronger."

Birmingham City vs Swansea City - prediction, team news, lineups

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

By Carter White | 05 Nov 2026 18:28

Fighting to move into the top two before the November international break, in-form Birmingham City host fellow promotion hopefuls Swansea City at St Andrew's @ Knighthead Park on Saturday afternoon.

Blues blitzed past Millwall in the Second City on Tuesday night, whilst the Swans' away-day issues continued, losing late on against a Portsmouth side who are pushing for a playoff position.

For the first time since earning promotion to the Premier League in 2008-09, Birmingham City have recorded three consecutive Championship home victories without conceding following Tuesday's battering of Millwall.

Seemingly replacing Tomoki Iwata as Blues' long-range specialist, Alex Cochrane fired in a second thunderbolt in the space of four days to open the scoring against the Lions, with Max Bird and Jay Stansfield also getting on the scoresheet in an impressive first period.

Winning five of their past six second-tier matches, Chris Davies's side have flown up to third spot in the Championship standings, just a single point behind West Midlands rivals Wolverhampton Wanderers in second.

Earning his maiden start for Birmingham, summer signing Bird made up for an injury-interrupted opening to his Second City career, starring with a goal and an assist in Tuesday's Millwall demolition.

Blues extended their unbeaten league run at St Andrew's this season to seven matches (W3 D4), with Saturday's hosts winning each of their past three home games by an aggregate scoreline of 10-0.

The surprise leaders at the beginning of the season, Swansea City are enduring their first major dip of a promising promotion charge, with the Welsh outfit losing three of their past four Championship contests.

There was more agony on the road for Vitor Matos's troops on Tuesday night, when added-time goals from Terry Devlin and Thomas Waddingham secured a 3-1 success for Portsmouth at Fratton Park.

Collecting just three points from their most recent quartet of fixtures, Swansea have dropped down to fifth place in the Championship table ahead of this weekend's clash in Birmingham, level on points with Blues.

Scoring the Swans' momentary leveller at Pompey earlier in the week, Zan Vipotnik has experienced a strange start to the season, with last term's Championship Golden Boot winner playing second fiddle to Adam Idah up front.

The Slovenian was a second-half substitute when Swansea and Birmingham clashed at the start of August in South Wales, where a solitary August Priske strike secured first-round EFL Cup progression for Davies's men.

Missing Birmingham's win over Millwall due to a knock, leading goalscorer Priske is a doubt to feature on Saturday afternoon.

Should the Danish marksman be unavailable, Blues should stick with Bird at the 10 position and Stansfield leading the line.

Dael Fry and Paik Seung-ho are nearing a return to action, although first-choice goalkeeper James Beadle is expected to miss out this weekend.

Swansea defender Stephen Welsh was forced off following a nasty collision at Fratton Park, however, the Scot's issue is not as bad as initially feared.

The 26-year-old is still a major doubt to play at Birmingham, though, with Ben Cabango likely to join Jenson Seelt at centre-back.

The Swans will be boosted by the return of attackers Eom Ji-Sung and Moussa Yeo for the Second City trip.

Birmingham City possible starting lineup:

Allsop; Laird, Neumann, Klarer, Cochrane; Solis, Iwata; Roberts, Bird, Millar; Stansfield

Vigouroux; Lissah, Seelt, Cabango, Tymon; Franco, Fulton, Stamenic; Opoku, Just, Vipotnik

Unbeaten on home soil in the Championship this season, Birmingham are a real force in front of their St Andrew's faithful.

Swansea have struggled massively in recent away matches but should bounce back to claim a point on Saturday.

Ex-Man United star Clayton Blackmore is forced to apologise for encouraging Mason Greenwood to 'come home to us' after winger beat Liverpool with Fenerbahce

📅 2026-11-05  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

By BEN WILLCOCKS, ASSISTANT SPORTS EDITOR

Published: 13:06 EDT, 5 November 2026 | Updated: 13:28 EDT, 5 November 2026

Former Manchester United footballer Clayton Blackmore has apologised after being criticised on social media for asking Mason Greenwood to 'come home' to Old Trafford.

Greenwood, 25, played a pivotal role for Fenerbahce during their 3-2 victory over Manchester United's archrivals Liverpool in the Champions League on Wednesday, scoring the Turkish giants' first-half equaliser.

The English forward broke through the ranks as a youngster at Manchester United and looked destined to become a key long-term player, but his career spiralled after he was arrested and charged in 2022 with suspected attempted rape, controlling and coercive behaviour and assault causing actual bodily harm.

Although those charges were subsequently dropped by the Crown Prosecution Service in February 2023, United decided Greenwood could not continue to ply his trade at Old Trafford after undertaking an internal six-month investigation.

Since his exit, which came after disgruntled Manchester United supporters groups heaped pressure on the club to cut ties with the forward, Greenwood has represented Spanish club Getafe on loan, French giants Marseille and Turkish side Fenerbahce, who he joined this summer for a reported €39million (£33.27million).

Blackmore, who played for Manchester United between 1982 and 1994, was met with backlash on Thursday after he heaped praise on Greenwood for his role in Fenerbahce's 3-2 win over Liverpool.

Mason Greenwood (centre) celebrates after scoring in Fenerbahce's 3-2 win over Liverpool

Former Man United star Clayton Blackmore pleaded for Grenwood to 'come home to us'

The 62-year-old Welshman apologised after his post was met with a furious backlash online

Greenwood's Manchester United career ended after he was charged with attempted rape, controlling and coercive behaviour and assault causing actual bodily harm in 2022

'Well done Mason Greenwood beating Liverpool. Come home to us please,' the Welshman, 62, posted on X.

Two hours later, after receiving a slew of disparaging comments on the platform, he said: 'Sorry I've offended anyone but I heard he had been cleared of any wrongdoing I apologise to everyone I've upset with my comments like I said I thought he had been cleared of everything and he’s had a baby with his girlfriend. Big sorry.'

Blackmore had previously come under fire for expressing his sadness that both Greenwood and Angel Gomes, another former English prodigy who was sold to Lille by Manchester United in 2020, no longer play for the club.

When the duo were both playing at Marseille last season, Blackmore said on X: 'Good to see Angel Gomez and Mason Greenwood score Marseille in first 26 mins. Sad they're not still at United.'

After landing himself in hot water with the post, he clarified to those commenting: 'Sorry I understand what you're saying but sometimes everything you hear is not always the truth why did his girlfriend stay with him I'm just looking at the football side.'

He later doubled down and wrote: 'Sorry didn't see or hear any of that I will do my home work next time just looking at it from the football side sorry if I've offended anyone especially his girlfriend and her family.'

While there was noisy resistance to Greenwood joining Marseille in the summer of 2025, he found no such opposition in Turkey, where one prominent journalist claimed 95 per cent of the population would not know or care about his past.

He didn't face any questions about the dropped charges or his public image in his first press conference and outside of brief post-match comments, has not conducted any interviews of note.

Greenwood has been hiding from the limelight a record signing would usually bask in, especially in the football-crazed city of Istanbul.

He found a home in a district called Sarıyer, a favourite among footballers, who largely live in gated communities.

Greenwood is said to count team-mates N'Golo Kante and Marco Asensio as neighbours and was seen in a rare online post after securing his new home, posing with the all-female team of estate agents who helped him with the purchase.

The former United starlet completed his move to Turkish giants Fenerbahce in the summer

Blackmore had previously come under fire for expressing his sadness at Greenwood leaving

Since the big-money move, Greenwood has only posted a handful of times on his Instagram profile, all of which were generic action shots after matches.

His latest offering came after Fenerbahce's stunning win over Liverpool, as he posted a gallery of photographs celebrating in the club's yellow and black jersey.

His partner, Harriet, 26, is also selective with how much she shares from their new life.

She only has four posts in total, with around 20 photos in each 'round-up', offering a glimpse into the high-life, though Greenwood himself only appears twice.

One of the images is of him looking exhausted in hospital at the birth of their second daughter in 2023 and another shows him holding hands with their eldest, Summer, who is three.
